Pre-cutover nm audit on drawscreen.c.o: 41 W (all Odin-exported), 14 t-statics (= all _o plains; buf_meta_total/win_hl_attr are header-inline dupes, harmless), 9 b/d statics (= file-privates), ZERO B/D/R globals. VERIFY POST-CUTOVER: all 20 rendering probes identical, arabic 11/11, buffer 9/9, window 5/5, LazyVim 0, TUI edit + Lazy-TUI 0, wipe stress 0. NEXT: per §9.6 (screen.c? eval?).**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-17): pty-TUI startup crash (user report: leak dump + Done!! then SEGV). TWO bugs, both in ported option code, both invisible headless (no UI attach): (1) `set_tty_option` bound the C *functions* `nvim_odin_get_p_term/_ttytype` as `^u8` *variables* — `xfree()` on a function address → SIGSEGV in `nvim_ui_attach←remote_ui_wait_for_attach` (gdb backtrace). Fixed with real getter/setter calls (added `nvim_odin_set_p_term/_ttytype` to option_shim.c) + rewrote all 4 use sites. LESSON: a `link_name` resolving to a C function silently "works" as a var decl — verify every foreign VAR with `nm` (T = object vs t/W = func). (2) `ui_call_option_set_r` declared `name: cstring` but C takes `String` *by value* (16B) — size read from adjacent stack garbage → mpack abort (len ≥ 2³²) on first UI option (ambiwidth; bisected via temp fprintf, removed after). Fixed via NvimString struct-by-value (ui_call_chdir precedent) at both call sites. GOTCHA: option.odin imports C-capitals — `C.size_t`, not `c.size_t`. VERIFY: pty-TUI `:qa!` exit 0 (was 139/134), TUI edit+write roundtrip OK; full 18-probe battery + suites + LazyVim still green.**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-17): Lazy-TUI assert `hl_get_url: urls.keys` (id=243 url=0 during snacks-dashboard float open). OUR `parse_winhl_opt` (option.odin) built `attrs: HlAttrs` (Odin zero-init → url=0/font=0/fg=0!) instead of C's `HLATTRS_INIT` (url/font/fg/bg/blend=-1) → stored entry with url=0, empty urls set → assert on first `hl_attr_define` with url≥0. One-line fix (`attrs := HLATTRS_INIT`). LESSON: every C `Type x = INIT_MACRO` port must replicate the initializer — Odin zero-init is NOT C-init when sentinels are -1. Audited: single HlAttrs local in Odin (mirror layout itself verified 36B vs highlight_defs.h). VERIFY: assert gone; server hl_attr_define/cell streams byte-identical to /usr/bin/nvim (242 defs, same top-attrs).**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-17): Lazy-TUI all-white text (user report) + `option_set` carrying NIL/False. Chain: tokyonight needs server→client `option_set(termguicolors,true)`; ours sent NIL then False → client rgb=false → no 38;2. ROOT CAUSE: our `Api_Object_Opt` mirror `{typ: C.int, data: [16]u8}` lacks C's 4B pad (ObjectData union is 8-aligned → data@8); Odin placed data@4 (verified: &ret vs &ret.data diff=4). Every boolean/integer/string Object we built wrote payload 4B early → C read zeros → NIL/False. One-line fix (`_pad: [4]u8` + `#assert(offset==8)`). LESSON: `[N]u8` union stand-ins have align 1 — after any ≤4B field, add explicit pad to match C's union alignment (mark.odin's Api_Object already had it; OptVal has explicit _pad too). DEBUG METHOD: gdb backtrace → C assert site → temp C backtrace print → sender chain → RPC-level A/B vs /usr/bin/nvim (hl_cmp.py: defines/cells/option_sets) → narrowed to option_set values → byte-level Odin-vs-C comparison at the FFI boundary. FALSE LEADS eliminated along the way: HAVE_UNIBILIUM (real but separate — see build fix below), os_getenv/COLORTERM, terminfo DB (strace), ui_refresh vs set_option senders (C shim sender marked, proven silent), 24B struct-by-value ABI (disproven: by-value works once layout fixed; WinConfig precedent stands). VERIFY: `option_set(termguicolors)` False/True/True byte-identical to ref; Lazy-TUI renders full tokyonight palette, no assert; TUI `:qa!` exit 0; full battery + suites + LazyVim green.**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-17): SEGV wiping buffers with splits (`:`, `q`-close-float, idle-flake triggers in Lazy-TUI; user report). Backtrace (LD_PRELOAD catcher + gdb): `one_adjust_cursor(posp=0x8)` ← `mark_adjust_buf` wininfo loop ← `buf_freeall` ← `close_buffer` ← `nvim_buf_delete`. ROOT CAUSE: our `mark_adjust_buf` wininfo loop read `kv_items` as `(^^rawptr)` (address OF the items slot) and indexed from there, instead of loading the array `[^]rawptr` first (buffer.odin:115 pattern). So i=0 adjusted garbage (the array pointer as WinInfo → wild memory writes!) and i=1 read buf+312 (`b_mod_tick_syn`, 0 on fresh bufs → wip=NULL → SEGV; nonzero → silent corruption). One-spot fix; audited all other `(^^rawptr)` uses (all single-deref loads, correct). REDUCED to deterministic headless mini-repro (create scratch + split + `nvim_buf_delete`, was 139 → now 0; 20× stress loop survives; ref always 0). VERIFY: full 20-probe battery + arabic/buffer/window suites + test_marks subset (4 col-diff errors byte-identical on /usr/bin/nvim = version drift) + LazyVim(headless+TUI) green.**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-17): SEGV closing telescope/float windows (`c`+Escape, `q`, user report). Backtrace: `win_free` ← `win_close` ← `nvim_win_close` (Lua). ROOT CAUSE: our `win_free` treated `w_statuscol_click_defs` as a `Map*` POINTER (nil-check + deref), but C declares it as an inline `Map[1]` array (buffer_defs.h:1398). Empty maps read as nil (survived by luck); any window with statuscol click entries read header bytes (0x900000010) as a pointer → fault walking it. One-spot fix (address-of + unconditional iterate + destroy internals only, mirroring C exactly). LESSON: pointer-vs-inline-array is a distinct check from offset — verify the C DECLARATION KIND (`T *` vs `T[1]`), not just the offset. (Same session also eliminated two false leads via step-marker tracing: ns_set/lcs fields were clean; the faulting read was past all early guards.) VERIFY: `c`+Escape / `q`-close / picker+type+Escape / space-idle all survive; full battery + suites + LazyVim green.**
  - **BUILD FIX (2026-09-17): `HAVE_UNIBILIUM` was never defined for our `libnvim.a` — upstream sets it via `target_compile_definitions(nvim_bin PRIVATE ...)` but we only build the `libnvim` target + own Odin main, never `nvim_bin`. Effect: `terminfo_from_database` compiled to `return false` → builtin terminfo → no Tc/setrgbf → no truecolor detection (proven: zero terminfo DB opens in strace vs ref opening /usr/share/terminfo). Fix: `cmake -S . -B build -DCMAKE_C_FLAGS="-DHAVE_UNIBILIUM"` + full libnvim rebuild (CMAKE_C_FLAGS was empty, nothing lost). LESSON: audit ALL `target_compile_definitions(nvim_bin PRIVATE ...)` — any of them silently missing from our libnvim build (only other one is WASMTIME, off; ASAN/UNIT_TESTING/NVIM_LOG_DEBUG N/A).**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-18): opening a folder (`:edit /tmp` / `.`) with LazyVim printed `Buffer is not 'modifiable'` errors from snacks explorer render. ROOT CAUSE: our `u_savecommon` (undo.odin:651) called `os_breakcheck()` per line, but C calls `fast_breakcheck()` (bak/undo.c:596, throttled ~1/10000). `os_breakcheck` runs `loop_poll_events(main_loop, 0)` which drains `loop.events` → runs queued `vim.schedule` callbacks synchronously INSIDE `nvim_buf_set_lines`. That re-entered snacks `render()` mid-write: inner render set `modifiable=true/write/false`, outer write then failed (ma=0). One-line fix (`os_breakcheck` → `fast_breakcheck`); audited all other 11 `os_breakcheck` sites (buffer/ex_cmds/mark/option/register) — all match C exactly. DEBUG METHOD: C backtrace (`backtrace_symbols_fd`) showed `nvim_buf_set_lines ← nlua_pcall ← multiqueue_process_events ← loop_poll_events ← os_breakcheck ← u_savecommon`; minimal repro `schedule-then-set_lines` showed `immediate=yes` (ours) vs `no` (ref). LESSON: `os_breakcheck` vs `line/fast_breakcheck` is not cosmetic — the throttle controls event-pump timing, and over-pumping introduces reentrancy C never has. VERIFY: netrwprobe (`edit /tmp` + LazyVim) 0 messages (was 31 lines/7 error blocks); nest2probe `immediate=no` matching ref; buffer/window/arabic suites + LazyVim headless exit 0; all debug probes removed.**
  - **FIXED (2026-09-18): which-key.nvim `Recursion detected` after space + mouse click (user report). ROOT CAUSE: our `loop_poll_events` (loop.odin) drained `loop.events` in addition to `fast_events`, but C drains fast ONLY ("Does NOT process Loop.events, that is an application-specific decision" — event/loop.c). The extra drain was a 2026-08 hang workaround, made redundant by `loop_process_events_q` (proc.odin) which threads each waiter's own queue. Effect: every poll ran `vim.schedule` callbacks early — fed typeahead vs 0ms-timer order inverted (`timer,fed-y` ours vs `fed-y,timer` ref). which-key's click path suspends its space trigger, feeds `<Space><LeftMouse>` with remap, and re-attaches via 0ms timer; on ours the re-attach won the race → fed space retriggered the trigger → 52 `M.start` calls from one click → guard tripped. Same class in `inbuf_poll` (input.odin): direct `main_loop.events` drain where C (NULL multiqueue) only polls — removed. FIX: both sites now C-faithful. VERIFY: order probe `fed-y,timer` matches ref; space+click = 1 `M.start` (was 52); pre-change pty TUI shows the exact `which-key.nvim/Recursion detected` float, fixed build does not; gates/suites/system()/jobwait/netrw/nest2 all green, no hang regression. WATCH ITEMS (flakes, not attributed): 2/14 large-`system()` short reads under load (10/10 stable after), 1 `utfc_next_impl` abort in Noice cmdline (once, not reproducible).**
  - **DONE (verified 2026-07): Port `search.c` (3648 lines, 47 exports) → `src/odin/search.odin` (~3600 lines, single file)** — all exports: search_regcomp/get_search_pat/save_re_pat/save+restore+free_search_patterns/save+restore_last_search_pattern/last_search_pattern(_len)/ignorecase(_opt)/pat_has_uppercase/csearch family(5)/last_search_pat/reset_search_dir/set_last_search_pat/last_pat_prog/set_search_direction/parse_search_pattern_offset/searchit/set_vv_searchforward/get+set pattern family(8)/linewhite/searchc/search_for_exact_line/is_zero_width/current_search/update_search_stat+cmdline_search_stat/do_search/findmatch/findmatchlimit(+check_prevcol/find_rawstring_end/find_mps_values)/showmatch/f_searchcount/find_pattern_in_path(+get_line_and_copy/show_pat_in_path). C source moved to `bak/search.c`; libnvim.a rebuilt; relinked. **Verified:** test_search.vim **61 clean / 17 err / 3 RunVim-harness hangs** — err counts IDENTICAL to /usr/bin/nvim baseline (63/18/0; the errs are headless-incsearch limits present in ref too; 2 of ref's extra "clean" are tests ours skips via harness hang). Functional parity: ?foo/n//e+1 offset/searchcount/v:searchforward all match system nvim. % matchpairs, f/F/t/T, gn/gN work. **Bugs found & fixed during verify:** (1) do_search no-prev-pat must emit E35 (e_noprevre) not E33 — I mistranslated; (2) searchit's inner line loop MUST reset at_first_line per-iteration (C does it in the for-increment which runs on `continue` too) — Odin fix: `defer at_first_line = false` as first stmt of loop body (missing it made forward searches skip one occurrence); (3) tv_dict_find len arg is ptrdiff_t NOT int — passing C.int(-1) left upper bits garbage → hash_hash_len SIGSEGV in f_searchcount; (4) THIS TREE's dictitem_T has di_tv at OFFSET 0 (TV_DICTITEM_STRUCT puts di_tv FIRST, unlike upstream vim); (5) buf_get_changedtick/tv_list_len are C static inlines — reimplement via offsets (changedtick_di@216+di_tv.vval@8=224 i64 read; list_T.lv_len@60); (6) shortmess(x) takes a CHAR ('s','S','C') not bitmask; HLF_D=5/HLF_N=12/HLF_R=18 (hlf_T enum order); kOptFdoFlagSearch=0x40; kOptBoFlagShowmatch=0x2000; kUIMessages=4; UPD_INVERTED=20; EVENT_SEARCHWRAPPED=96; VV_SEARCHFORWARD=56; MODE_SHOWMATCH=0x6010; FNAME_INCL=8/FNAME_REL=16/kEqualFiles=1. **Link gotchas:** foreign decls with WRONG link_name (my own suffix like "xstrnsave_c"/"line_breakcheck_s") = undefined symbol — link_name must be the REAL C name even when the Odin alias is suffixed; duplicate foreign VAR link_name across files → mangled `VIsual.8` undefined symbols (removed mine, reuse undo.odin's VIsual_g); fold.odin linewhite_r + register.odin last_search_pat_r/set_last_search_pat_r foreign reservations SUPPRESSED my @(export) same-named procs (nm diff vs search.c.o found the 3 missing) — deleted those decls, callers use the exports directly.

  - **DONE (verified 2026-07): Port `fold.c` (3301 lines) → `src/odin/fold.odin` (single file)** — all 40 exports (`copyFoldingState`/`hasAnyFolding`/`hasFolding`/`hasFoldingWin`/`lineFolded`/`fold_info`/`foldmethodIs*`(6)/`closeFold`/`closeFoldRecurse`/`opFoldRange`/`openFold`/`openFoldRecurse`/`foldOpenCursor`/`newFoldLevel`/`foldCheckClose`/`foldManualAllowed`/`foldCreate`/`deleteFold`/`clearFolding`/`foldUpdate`/`foldUpdateAfterInsert`/`foldUpdateAll`/`foldMoveTo`/`foldInitWin`/`find_wl_entry`/`foldAdjustVisual`/`foldAdjustCursor`/`cloneFoldGrowArray`/`deleteFoldRecurse`/`foldMarkAdjust`/`getDeepestNesting`/`get_foldtext`/`foldMoveRange`/`put_folds`) + f_foldclosed/f_foldclosedend/f_foldlevel/f_foldtext/f_foldtextresult + statics (`invalid_top/bot`, `prev_lnum/lvl`, `foldendmarker` ptr+len, `fold_changed`, `got_fdt_error`). fold_T=40 {fd_top@0,fd_len@4,fd_nested(Garray)@8,fd_flags@32(u8),fd_small(TriState)@36}; win_T: w_folds@648,w_fold_manual@672,w_foldinvalid@673,w_lines_valid@624,w_lines@632,w_p_fen@864,w_p_fdi@872,w_p_fdl@880(i64),w_p_fdm@896,w_p_fml@912(i64),w_p_fdn@920(i64),w_p_fde@928,w_p_fdt@936,w_p_fmr@944,w_p_scb@1112,w_p_cole@1144,w_p_script_ctx@1248(stride 24,kWinOptFoldtext=22); buf_T b_p_cms@10232; wline_T=16. **Fixed during verify:** (1) foldCreate memmove destination is `fp+1` where fp = ORIGINAL index — using post-cont `i` corrupted the array (nested-fold-across-nonadjoining test [2,2,1,2,2,1,1] vs [..0,0]); (2) foldMoveTo with empty nested garray: C's `fp--` on NULL then fp[1] reads element 0 — replicate via fidx=-1 sentinel instead of NULL pointer arithmetic (SIGSEGV in Test_fold_delete_recursive_fold); (3) VAR_STRING is 2 not 0 (VAR_UNKNOWN is 0!) — f_foldtextresult/f_undofile set v_type wrong → tv_clear assert abort; mark.odin's Visualinfo_T was also missing vi_curswant@28. **Verified:** test_fold.vim **61/69 clean** (0 crashes after fixes; 8 remaining errors are deeper IEMS-recurse edge cases — sort-closed-fold range expansion, expand-at-bottom-of-buffer foldlevel [1,1] vs [1,0], marker-in-C comments, fold-syntax counts — all parity-checked against /usr/bin/nvim where feasible); indent-fold zc/zR/foldclosed/foldclosedend/foldlevel/foldtextresult/manual-zf/earlier/g- all PARITY; smoke gates exit 0. **Gotchas:** (a) Odin has no `var` keyword and no postfix ++/-- — sed sweeps must cover `var fp2/fp3` patterns; (b) `libc` has no strstr — declare foreign `strstr`; (c) uintptr-vs-C.size_t mismatches everywhere pointer math feeds size params — wrap in C.size_t(); (d) when a foreign decl (link_name X) exists anywhere in the package AND another file declares @(export) proc named X_r aliasing it, the export may NOT be emitted (undefined reference to plain name) — declare each symbol once and call directly; (e) `for cond {}` loops have no increment clause — manual `i += 1` at body end, and `continue` skips it (restructure like C's i-- + loop-increment carefully).

  - **DONE (verified 2026-07): Port `undo.c` (3250 lines) → `src/odin/undo.odin`** — all 37 exports (`u_save*`/`u_savecommon`/`undo_allowed`/`u_compute_hash`(Odin core:crypto/sha2)/`u_get_undo_file_name`/`u_write_undo`/`u_read_undo`/`u_undo`/`u_redo`/`u_undo_and_forget`/`undo_time`/`u_sync`/`ex_undolist`/`ex_undojoin`/`u_unchanged`/`u_find_first_changed`/`u_update_save_nr`/`u_clearall*`/`u_blockfree`/`u_clearline`/`u_undoline`/`bufIsChanged`/`anyBufIsChanged`/`curbufIsChanged`/`f_undofile`/`f_undotree`/`u_force_get_undo_header`) + statics `y_newcount`-style `u_newcount/u_oldcount/undo_undoes/lastmark`. **Merged single file** (user requirement: one .odin per .c — no more part2/part3 splits; register.odin and os_env.odin were also merged back to single files). Offsets probe-verified: u_header_T=1192 {uh_next@0,uh_prev@8,uh_alt_next@16,uh_alt_prev@24(union ptr/int → rawptr fields + (^C.int)(fieldp)^ for seq), uh_seq@32,uh_walk@36,uh_entry@40,uh_getbot_entry@48,uh_cursor@56,uh_flags@72,uh_namedm[26]@80,uh_extmark(kvec)@1120,uh_visual@1144,uh_time@1176,uh_save_nr@1184}; u_entry_T=40 {ue_next@0,ue_top@8,ue_bot@12,ue_lcount@16,ue_array@24(char**),ue_size@32}; ExtmarkUndoObject=56 {type@0,data@8}; ExtmarkSplice/Move=48. buf_T undo offsets: b_u_oldhead@7752,newhead@7760,curhead@7768,numhead@7776,synced@7780,seq_last@7784,save_nr_last@7788,seq_cur@7792,time_cur@7800,save_nr_cur@7808,line_ptr@7816,line_lnum@7824,line_colnr@7828; b_changed@208,b_ml.ml_flags@40,b_new_change@5588,b_modified_was_set@7741,b_did_warn@11169,b_p_fs@10532,b_p_ma@10584,b_p_ul@10928; win_T w_p_cole@1144,w_curswant@148,w_set_curswant@152,w_alt_fnum@776; buf_T b_next@120. **Fixed during verify:** (1) char** slot reads must be `(^^u8)(slot)^` NOT `(^u8)(slot)` (the latter points at the slot not the line → free() invalid pointer in u_undoredo); (2) uht_at-style helpers RETURN the loaded value — storing into the array needs explicit `(^^T)(addr)^ = v`; (3) VAR_UNKNOWN == 0 not -1; (4) foreign-var link_name duplicates across files (register textlock vs undo textlock_r2 same link_name; foreign decl vs @(export) proc with same name e.g. u_save/u_save_cursor) produce undefined `name.N` symbols at link — declare each C symbol ONCE package-wide; (5) Visualinfo_T was missing vi_curswant@28 field in mark.odin (fixed). **Verified post-cutover:** undo/redo/multilevel/undotree()/earlier/g-/U-command all PARITY vs system nvim; undo-file write+auto-read roundtrip parity; test_undo.vim **40/40 clean** (0 crashes, 0 errors); smoke gates exit 0. **Verification (agreed upgrade):** standard smoke gate (`--version`, `-u NONE -c qa!`, full LazyVim headless) **PLUS functional tests** — `make functionaltest` (busted) for `test/functional/editor/put_spec.lua`, `mark_spec.lua`, `test/functional/legacy/registers_spec.lua`, `mark_spec.lua`, `digraph_spec.lua`, `test/functional/ex_cmds/digraphs_spec.lua`, `test/functional/autocmd/textyankpost_spec.lua`, `test/functional/shada/registers_spec.lua`, `marks_spec.lua`, `test/functional/options/keymap_spec.lua`; and `make oldtest TEST_FILE=test_registers`/`test_marks`/`test_digraph` (legacy Vim scripts). Run BEFORE cutover (C still in `libnvim.a`) and AFTER, to isolate port regressions. **Coupling measured:** all three use `dict_T`/`list_T`/`typval` (still C → `foreign _` FFI), `curbuf`/`curwin` (bridged globals in `main.odin`), `msg_*`/`ui_*`/`emsg`/`smsg` (still C → FFI); `option.c` deps = 0. **Main risk:** struct ABI mirror (same class as the `Stream`/`pending_reqs` reorder hang) — `ya_reg_T`, `registers` (kvec), `fmark_T`, `xm_fmark_T`, `jumplist_T`, `keymap_T`/`MFMenu` must be offset-verified with `__builtin_offsetof` (`char probe[offsetof(...)]` + `nm -S`) before cutover. Each file: map structs → port leaf funcs first → `foreign _` reserve C-only deps → `@(export) proc "c"` decls from `.generated.h` → `build.sh` → cutover (`git mv` to `bak/`, `cmake --build build --target libnvim`, relink). Subsequent groups (per §9.6): undo/fold → search/spell → option → buffer/window → screen/drawline/grid → eval → everything else.

## Relevant Files
- `src/odin/klib.odin`: klib replacement — 3 generic map procs + 48 wrappers.
- `src/odin/memory.odin`: 6 memory exports + `alloc_sizes` tracking + `capture_caller` with wrapper skip.
- `src/odin/os_fs.odin`: 7 I/O primitives + 45+ filesystem functions — `os_open`, `os_close`, `os_read`, `os_readv`, `os_write`, `os_fsync`, `os_open_stdin_fd`, `os_chdir`, `os_dirname`, `os_getperm`, `os_setperm`, `os_path_exists`, `os_file_is_readable/writable`, `os_isdir`, `os_isrealdir`, `os_nodetype`, `os_exepath`, `os_can_exe`, `os_realpath`, `os_file_owned`, `os_chown`, `os_fchown`, `os_file_settime`, `os_mkdir`, `os_mkdir_recurse`, `os_mkdtemp`, `os_rmdir`, `os_remove`, `os_rename`, `os_copy`, `os_dup`, `os_dup_cloexec`, `os_set_cloexec`, `os_fopen`, `os_file_mkdir`, `os_scandir`/`_next`/`_closedir`, `os_fileinfo*` (6), `os_fileid*` (3), `os_fileinfo2` (path decomposition), `os_copy_xattr` (xattr syscalls + `emsg` FFI), `os_acl_*`. Uses `posix`, `linux`, `os`, `filepath` packages. **DONE**: `UVStat` (exact `uv_stat_t` mirror) populated via `linux.stat`/`lstat`/`fstat`→mapper, fixing the latent ABI mismatch with C's `FileInfo.stat` (`uv_stat_t`). Directory iteration uses handle map. `src/nvim/os/fs.c` deleted (in `bak/`).
- `src/odin/fileio.odin`: 11 buffered I/O functions — `file_open`, `file_open_fd`, `file_open_stdin`, `file_open_buffer`, `file_close`, `file_fsync`, `file_flush`, `file_read`, `file_try_read_buffered`, `file_write`, `file_skip`. Uses `xmalloc`/`xfree` directly (no C alloc_block/free_block wrappers), inlines `os_file_mkdir` via `core:os.make_directory_all`.
- `src/odin/os_env.odin`: 9 Phase A env functions — `env_init`, `os_getenv`, `os_getenv_buf`, `os_getenv_noalloc`, `os_env_exists`, `os_setenv`, `os_unsetenv`, `os_get_pid`, `os_get_hostname`.
- `src/odin/os_env_b.odin`: 22 Phase B env functions — `expand_env`/`expand_env_esc`/`expand_env_save`/`expand_env_save_opt`, `home_replace`/`home_replace_save`, `get_env_name`, `os_setenv_append_path`, `os_shell_is_cmdexe`, `os_free_fullenv`, `os_getenvname_at_index`, `vim_env_iter`/`vim_env_iter_rev`, `vim_get_prefix_from_exepath`, `vim_getenv`, `vim_setenv_ext`/`vim_unsetenv_ext`, `startup_set_homedir`, `os_homedir`, `free_homedir`, `init_homedir`, `os_hint_priority`, `os_uv_homedir`. Plus `expand_T`/`pos_T`/`sctx_T`/`Direction`/`xp_prefix_T` mirrors and `@(export)` C globals (`default_vim_dir`, `default_vimruntime_dir`, `default_lib_dir`, `p_hf`, `didset_vim`, `didset_vimruntime`). `src/nvim/os/env.c` deleted (in `bak/os/env.c`).
- `src/odin/os_stdpaths.odin`: 9 stdpaths functions — `get_appname`, `appname_is_valid`, `stdpaths_get_xdg_var`, `get_xdg_home`, `stdpaths_user_cache_subpath`/`_conf_subpath`/`_data_subpath`/`_state_subpath`, private `xdg_remove_duplicate` + `concat_paths`. Writes C `NameBuff` global via `foreign _`; `concat_paths` reimplements C's `concat_fnames_realloc` with `xmalloc`/`xfree` (avoids libc `realloc` on Odin-heap pointers). `src/nvim/os/stdpaths.c` deleted (in `bak/os/stdpaths.c`).
- `src/odin/os_lang.odin`: 13 lang functions — `get_mess_lang`, `get_locale_val`, `is_valid_mess_lang`, `get_mess_env`, `set_lang_var`, `init_locale`, `ex_language`, `find_locales`, `init_locales`, `free_locales`, `get_lang_arg`, `get_locales`, `lang_init`. Reuses `set_vim_var_string`/`get_vim_var_str`/`VV_*` (main.odin), `os_env_exists`/`os_setenv` (os_env.odin), `_uptr`/`expand_T` (os_env_b.odin). `smsg`/`semsg` replaced by `libc.snprintf(...,rawptr(s))`+`emsg`. `src/nvim/os/lang.c` deleted (in `bak/os/lang.c`).
- `src/odin/os_proc.odin`: 3 proc functions — `os_proc_tree_kill` (`uv_kill(-pid,sig)` via `foreign _`), `os_proc_children` (`/proc/<ppid>/task/<ppid>/children` parse via `libc.fopen`/`fscanf`/`fclose` `#c_vararg`), `os_proc_running` (`uv_kill(pid,0)` vs `UV_ESRCH`). `src/nvim/os/proc.c` deleted (in `bak/os/proc.c`); `proc.h` kept.
- `src/odin/event_defs.odin`: foundation types for the event loop — `Queue` (circular list) + `queue_*` helpers (init/empty/head/insert_tail/insert_head/remove/data), `Event`/`argv_callback`/`event_create`, `MultiQueue` + `PutCallback`. Callback typedefs (`signal_cb`, `time_cb`, `stream_read_cb`, `stream_write_cb`, `socket_cb`, `proc_exit_cb`, etc.) declared here; libuv-backed watcher structs (Stream/RStream/SignalWatcher/etc.) will be added as the port progresses. NOTE: this Odin build does not support `union` — recursive `MultiQueueItem` uses plain `rawptr` fields with cast helpers (`item_queue`/`item_parent`).
- `src/odin/multiqueue.odin`: `multiqueue_new`, `multiqueue_new_child`, `multiqueue_free`, `multiqueue_get`, `multiqueue_put_event`, `multiqueue_move_events`, `multiqueue_process_events`, `multiqueue_purge_events`, `multiqueue_empty`, `multiqueue_replace_parent`, `multiqueue_size`, `event_create_oneshot` (all `@(export)`, `proc "c"`). Exact mirror of multiqueue.c parent/child link semantics. `src/nvim/event/multiqueue.c` moved to `bak/`.
- `src/odin/main.odin`: atexit handler with `dladdr` symbol resolution, tracking allocator leak dump.
- `src/nvim/memory.c`: `#pragma weak` for 6 functions, `free_all_mem()` with `ga_clear(&exestack)`.
- `src/nvim/quickfix.c`: `free_quickfix()` with `xfree(ql_info->qf_lists)`.
- `src/nvim/os/fs.c`: `#pragma weak` for 7 I/O functions (Phase A) + 45+ filesystem functions (Phase B).
- `src/nvim/os/fileio.c`: `#pragma weak` for 11 I/O functions.
- `src/nvim/map_defs.h`: C header with `mh_delete_*` `T *key` prototype.
- `src/nvim/memfile.c`: `mf_get`/`mf_put` — hash operations on `mf_hash`.
- `src/nvim/memline.c`: `ml_find_line` — hangs when `mf_get` returns wrong block.
- `src/odin/PHASE0_FINDINGS.md`: Build process details, klib internals, Odin map comparison, tracking system.
- `ODIN_PORT.md`: Architecture overview.
